みずほ経済 EXPRESS

中国が住宅販売モデルを転換へ 
～不動産不況は脱却の兆しなお見えず～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 月岡直樹  
080-1069-6684 naoki.tsukioka@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 中国政府が新規則を発表。住宅は予約販売から完成後販売にシフト 
中国政府は8月28日、住宅販売制度と住宅ローン管理に関する新規則を発表した。新規の住宅不動産
プロジェクトについて完成後販売の方式を促すほか、未完成物件の予約販売における住宅ローンの実
行時期を物件引き渡し後に変更する。住宅の予約販売制度が不動産ディベロッパーの過度なレバレッ
ジ追求による資金繰り悪化と、それに伴う住宅引き渡し遅延問題を引き起こしたことから、住宅販売
モデルの転換を加速させる。 
住宅都市農村建設部や中国人民銀行、国家金融監督管理総局などの関連省庁が一斉に通達や管理規
定を公布した。住宅販売制度に関する通達では、①予約販売の代金（購入者が支払う頭金と銀行から
振り込まれる住宅ローン）について住宅不動産プロジェクトごとの専用口座を通じた銀行側による資
金管理を強化すること、②新たに不動産用地や建設許可を取得するプロジェクトに対して「完成物件
での販売を優先的に選択」させ、建設許可を取得済みの新規プロジェクトについても完成物件での販
売を奨励すること、③住宅不動産プロジェクトごとに会社を設立し、メインバンクを選定して開発資
金の融資を受けること、などの方針を示した。一方、住宅ローンの新たな管理規定には、予約販売に
おける住宅ローンについて「厳格にプロジェクト竣工届出後に実行しなければならない」と明記した
ほか、これまで最長30年とされてきた住宅ローンの借入期間を40年に変更することを盛り込んだ。 
■ 「保交房」に一定のメド。ディベロッパーの法的整理が進む可能性も 
中国の不動産市場はこれまで、ディベロッパーが物件の竣工を待たずに販売を開始する予約販売方
式が一般的であり、住宅ローンも購入者が予約購入した時点で実行され、返済義務が生じる慣行とな
っていた。予約販売には、購入者にとって不動産価格の値上がり前に物件を確保できるメリットが、
ディベロッパーにとって不動産開発に投じた資金を早期に回収できるメリットがあった。 
しかし、財務レバレッジの追求による収益の最大化を狙うディベロッパーは、回収した予約販売代
金を新たな不動産開発用地の獲得や不動産とは関係のないビジネスへの投資に流用し、事業を急拡大
させた。その実態は自転車操業であり、市場の過熱を懸念した中国政府が2021年にディベロッパーへ
の資金調達規制 （いわゆる 「3つのレッドライン」 ） を正式導入するとたちまち資金繰りに行き詰まり、
社債の債務不履行（デフォルト）が頻発する。ディベロッパーの信用力が低下したところにゼロコロ
2026 年 9 月 30 日

### Page 2

2

ナ政策の強化が追い打ちをかける形で販売が急減し、ディベロッパーの資金繰りが一段と悪化したこ
とで建設工事の進捗が滞り、2022年に入って予約販売済み物件の引き渡し遅延が深刻化する。 
ここに至って予約販売のデメリットが明らかとなる。すなわち、物件引き渡しリスクを負担するの
は購入者であり、たとえ遅延しても住宅ローンの返済を続けなければならないことである。事態を重
く見た中国政府は、「保交楼（予約販売済み未完成物件の引き渡し保証。「保交房」も同じ）」のス
ローガンを掲げ、ディベロッパーの資金繰り支援に乗り出す。2024年1月には、当局が融資適格と判断
した不動産開発プロジェクトをまとめたホワイトリストを作成し、各銀行がこのリストに基づいてデ
ィベロッパーに資金を融通するスキームを構築して、問題の解消を急がせたのである。 
中国政府がこのタイミングで新規則を発表した背景には、この「保交房」に一定のメドが付いたと
判断したことがあるとみられる。事実、国務院の李強首相は2026年3月の全人代における政府活動報告
で「『保交房』の任務は全面的に完成した」と宣言している。また、見逃せない動きとして、広東省
広州市の中級人民法院（地方裁判所）が8月21日、実質的に経営破綻している某ディベロッパーについ
て、債権者による清算の申し立てを受理したことが挙げられる。 
デフォルトを起こした不動産ディベロッパーの多くはこれまで、倒産によって債務の返済や予約販
売物件の引き渡し、建設業者への代金支払いなどの義務・責任を放棄することが許されず、債権者か
ら債務の減免や返済猶予の合意を得つつゾンビ化した状態で経営を続けてきた。ディベロッパーの破
綻が相次げば、不良債権問題の深刻化によって金融システムが動揺するリスクを招くだけでなく、予
約販売物件の引き渡しが困難となって社会問題に発展したり、下請け業者の連鎖倒産で景気が急激に
冷え込む可能性もあったからである。 
今回の動きは、清算の申し立てを裁判所が「受理」しただけではあり、実際に清算が進むのかはな
お予断を許さないが、中国政府がディベロッパーの法的整理を前に進める意思を示したとみることも
できる。今後、清算手続きが順調に進むのか、他のディベロッパーについても同様の動きが広がるの
かを注意深く見守る必要があろう。 
■ 住宅販売モデル転換の社会的意義は大きいが、不動産市況への影響は軽微 
では、住宅販売モデルの転換は不動産市場にどのような影響を与えるであろうか。その前にまず、
不動産市場の現状を整理しておきたい。 
資金調達規制の強化をきっかけとした不動産不況は6年目に突入した。中国の不動産市場規模（販売
金額）は2021～25年にかけて半分に縮小し、販売面積や開発投資額は4割、新規着工面積に至っては7
割も減少した（図表1）。2026年も不況脱却の兆しは見えず、1～8月の販売面積は前年比▲12.1％、開
発投資額は同▲19.9％と低迷が続いている。開発投資の先行指標とされる不動産開発向け銀行貸出も
同▲33.3％となっており、ディベロッパーにとって資金繰りが厳しい状況は変わっていない。 
販売が大きく落ち込んだことで、市場には過剰な在庫が積み上がっている。みずほ総合研究所調査
部の試算によれば、建設中の物件を含む中国の住宅在庫（商用およびオフィス用を除く）は2025年末
時点で40.8億平米に達する（図表2）。2025年の住宅販売面積が7.3億平米であったことから、新規着工
がゼロになると仮定しても在庫をすべて消化するのに5年以上かかる計算である。中国政府は2024年5
月、地方政府による住宅在庫の買い取り策を発表し、同年10月には地方政府が専項債（特別債）の発
行で得た資金を買い取りに使うことも容認したが、在庫の消化は遅々として進んでいない。

### Page 3

3

住宅価格（主要70都市平均）は2021年7月のピーク時から新築で▲14％、中古で▲23％下落し、全体
として下落傾向に歯止めがかかっていない。ただ、図表3のとおり、底堅い需要のある大都市（一級都
市：北京、上海、広州、深圳）と膨大な在庫を抱える地方都市（二級都市：各省の省都。三級都市：
省都以下の主要都市）で二極化が顕著になっている。特に上海は堅調で、その新築住宅価格は2021年
7月から＋24％も上昇している。とはいえ、その堅調さは資産としての不動産保有の魅力を有する一部
の都市に限ったものであり、市況の回復が地方都市にまで広く波及する様子はうかがえない。 
住宅引き渡し遅延問題の発生以降、引き渡しリスクのない完成物件の購入が増加しつつあるが、予
約販売は2025年時点でも依然として住宅販売面積全体の6割超を占めている（図表4）。住宅販売モデ
ルの転換、すなわち予約販売から完成後販売への移行と予約販売における住宅ローン実行の後ろ倒し
は、住宅購入者の物件引き渡しリスクを大幅に軽減することになり、消費者保護の観点からもその社
会的意義は非常に大きいといえる。しかし、これに伴ってディベロッパーの資金繰りはより厳しいも
のとなる。ディベロッパーの資金調達に占める販売代金（頭金などの前受金と住宅ローン）の比率は
4割を超えているからである（図表5）。このため、短期的には新規の開発（土地の取得や新規着工）
図表1 不動産指標（年次） 図表2 住宅在庫

4

が減少する可能性が高い。ただ、新規則は完成
後販売を全国一律に「強制」するのではなく、
あくまで「優先的に選択」させるとしているこ
とから、地方によってはディベロッパーの資金
繰りにも配慮した柔軟な対応がとられる可能
性もある。 
新規制が不動産市況に与える影響は、住宅ロ
ーンの最長借入期間の延長が新たな需要を掘
り起こすとしても、軽微であろう。不動産不況
を脱するためには、過剰在庫の消化とディベロ
ッパーの法的整理、不良債権の処理を着実に進
め、市場を健全な状態に戻すことが不可欠とい
えるが、それにはまだ相当の時間を要するもの
とみられる。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1300-1.1450

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.21-115.09.24 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.804% ~ 0.830%  
30天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95%  
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.84%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。 
週二央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.638%，略低於
市場預期，且較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.804%~0.830%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 及 5 年期指標券 A15108 均未成
交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Alchip Technologies
Better allocation into 2027E; N2 project on track
Q3 sales up 243% QoQ on Trainium 3 ramp
Alchip reported September sales of NT$9,979 mn, up 14% MoM and 496% YoY,
concluding Q326 sales of NT$26,188 mn for +243% QoQ and 299% YoY, slightly
ahead of consensus, although below UBSe as we assumed a higher wafer allocation for
the Trainium 3 project, while sales contribution for its Chinese automotive customer and

NRE might also have came in lower than our expectation. We now expect Alchip's Q326
GM to arrive at 20.1%, down from 34.8% in Q226 on less favourable mix, leading to
OPM of 15.9%. We forecast Q326 EPS to grow 132% QoQ to NT$46.34.
Trainium 3  to sustain longer with potential upside; Trainium 4 tape-out on track
We expect Alchip's Trainium 3 monthly shipment run-rate to retain at current level into
H127F, and could extend into H227F with potential better allocation for Alchip with a

similar margin profile, subject to the final negotiation with its customer. We think
Trainium 3 deployments could stretch a little bit longer with a limited gap (vs prior ~3
months) before the next-generation Trainium 4 launch given the 3nm capacity tightness.
We continue to forecast overall Trainium 3 shipments at ~1.8mn units for 2026 and will
increase to ~2.8 mn units in 2027E, up 55% YoY, although Alchip's Trainium 3 shipment
could outgrow at 80%+ YoY given potential better allocation. For the Trainium 4 project

(2nm node), management noted it remains on track for tape-out before year-end and
still targets mass production by late-2027 or early 2028. Alchip continues to believe it
could enjoy a higher value-add given the increasing design complexity on chiplet and
higher wafer pricing for the 2nm node, while GM could retain at a similar level
compared with the current N3 project.
Engaging new customers for diversification
Alchip noted it remains in talks with another major CSP customer for potential adoption

of COT (customer-owned tooling) business model in the future for further cost saving.
We believe Alchip's competitive pricing,  strength for faster turnaround, and advanced
packaging knowledge put Alchip in a  good position to capture this opportunity, if the
customer decides to change the business model. Nevertheless, we do not expect
meaningful revenue contribution in 2027-28E from the new CSP customer. On the other
hand, we believe Alchip is in talks with other HPC customers for mid-size physical design

projects and this is likely to become a new revenue stream from 2027E. We estimate it
could book US$200-300mn revenue in 2027E from the new HPC customer.
Valuation: Maintain Buy with NT$6,600 price target
We lower our 2026E EPS by 11% primarily on lower Trainium 3 and automotive
contribution. We raise 2027E/28E EPS by 2%/5% to reflect  revenue contribution from
new HPC customer, offset by higher opex. We keep our PT unchanged at NT$6,600,
based on the same 30x 2027E EPS (vs. a 15-50x PE range). Stock is trading at ~20x 2027

P/E, below its peers and past five year average of 30x.

Company Description
Alchip was founded in 2002 by a group of Cadence managers with 20+ years of experience in
the semiconductor industry. It is an IC design and production service provider, and
management believes it can leverage third-party IP to enable customized chipset solutions for

customers cost effectively and fast to market. The company was funded by Cisco Investment
and Softbank Investment with VC funding in early 2000, then had its IPO on the emerging
board in Taiwan in 2010 and the main board in 2014.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Alchip using PE methodology.
Downside risks include slower-than-expected advanced node project diversification and
ramp, and growing competition in advanced projects from other IC design service companies.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
Monthly Databook: Sep-26
Shipments -2% vs. MSe;
Lowering 4Qe by 3%
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao
Equity Analyst
Howard.Kao@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2989
Irene Yen
Equity Analyst
Irene.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2869
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst

Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Overall September ODM NB shipments came in 2% below MSe: Quarter-end pull-
ins drove notebook shipments up 32% m/m, although volumes were 2% below MSe,
with weakness mainly coming from Wistron and Compal, and to a lesser extent,
Pegatron, reflecting slower NB shipments from Dell, as Dell accounted for ~60% of

Compal's NB shipment, and ~55% of Wistron's in CY25, on our estimates. Thus total
3Q NB builds came in at 25.2M units (-17% q/q, -26% y/y). This sequential decline
is sub-seasonal vs. the 15Y average seasonality of +5% q/q in 3Q.
Pull-forward demand appears to have come to an end, channel partners and
distributors have stocked up on inventory, and OEMs are slowing their sales into the
channel. We believe this is why our conversations with the ODMs suggest a slightly

weaker 4Q volume outlook than previously expected, despite favorable mix shift.
Thus, we lower our 4Q NB build estimate by 3% to 23.1M (-8% q/q, -29% y/y).
This sequential decline is sub-seasonal (despite an already lowered base in 3Q) vs.
the 15Y average seasonality of flat q/q in 4Q. We forecast October NB builds at
6.8M (-30% m/m, -33% y/y). Our revised forecasts imply CY26 builds of ~107.8M
units (-16% y/y) for the top five Taiwan NB ODMs.

Key Takeaways
Top five ODMs' September 2026 NB shipments were 9.7M units (+32% m/m, -
20% y/y), 2% below our estimate.
Total 3Q26 NB builds came in at 25.2M (-17% q/q, -26% y/y).
We see October 2026 NB builds at 6.8M units (-30% m/m, -33% y/y).
We lower our 4Q26 NB build estimate by 3% to 23.1M units (-8% q/q, -29% y/y).
Our top five ODMs' CY26 NB build estimate is ~107.8M units (-16% y/y).

STOCK RATINGS

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

AVC (3017.TW): Liquid cooling rising penetration and AI servers ramp-up 
to drive growth ahead; Buy
19 October 2026 | 3:24AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue 
sequential growth through Dec 2026, leading 4Q26 revenues to grow at +42% YoY/ 
+15% QoQ, driven by: (1) new AI server racks ramp-up, driving the overall liquid 
cooling penetration rate. As we highlighted in Oct (report link), we expect AI server 
rack (NVL72-equivalent) shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 63k/ 110k/ 164k 
in 2026-28E, reflecting our positive view on AI server racks. (2) Its major ASIC AI 
server customer’s new model ramp-up, driving its air-cooling and liquid-cooling 
components, along with a rising liquid cooling penetration rate. (3) Its customer base 
expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further drive its liquid cooling components in 
4Q26. And (4) increasing adoption rate of ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit 
the lightweight design and major customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s 
net income to grow at a +39% CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a 
growing liquid cooling penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total 
solutions (air cooling, liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain 
Buy.

### Page 2

Earnings revisions: We factor in AVC’s Sep revenue and revise down our 2026E net 
income by -1%, mainly on lower revenues in 4Q26E, reflecting the slow consumer 
electronics under high memory costs and AI servers model transition. We revise up our 
2027-28E net income by 4% / 0%, mainly on higher revenues and GMs. Our 2027-28E 
revenue is revised up by 1% / 1%, mainly on a higher contribution from AI server liquid 
cooling components. 2027-28E GM are revised up to reflect the rising liquid cooling 
components driving profitability.

Valuation: We continue to use a near-term P/E to derive our 12-month target price and 
update our target P/E multiple to 30.6x 2027E EPS (vs. 30.3x previously), which is still 
derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple correlation. Our target P/E 
multiple is above AVC’s historical average +1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on AVC riding on the rising penetration rate of liquid-cooled AI servers. 
With our updated target P/E and earnings estimates, we raise our 12-month target price 
to NT$4,736 (from NT$4,513). Maintain Buy.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,736 on a target P/E multiple of 30.6x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

Document 203: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

00

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 
기다렸던 4 행정 중속 엔진 캐파 증설 단행 
HD현대중공업의 4행정 엔진 캐파는 기존 3.0GW에서 증설이 완료되는 '28년부터 
램프업을 거쳐 '30년 7.2GW까지 확대될 계획이다. ①[육상 발전용]은 신공장(울산 
온산) 3GW 와 HD 현대엔진(전남 영암) 1GW 를 합쳐 4.0GW, ②[선박용]은 
2.3GW에서 3.2GW로 확대된다. HD현대엔진 캐파는 기존 0.7GW에서 1GW까
지 확대되며, 해당 라인은 신공장이 가동되기 전 ‘27~’28 년 구간에서의 선박&육상 엔
진 모두 대응 가능하다. 신공장 캐파는 총 3.0GW로, ‘28년 약 30%의 가동률로 시
작해 램프업을 거쳐 ‘30 년부터 풀캐파로 20MW급 모델을 생산할 예정이다. 4 행정 
중속엔진에 대한 수요 가시성은 높다. 동사가 공유했던 육상 발전 엔진 인콰이어리 규
모는 기존 약 3~9GW였으나, 현재는 이를 크게 상회하는 수준으로 소통하고 있다.

최근 부유식 데이터센터(FDC)에 대한 하이퍼스케일러들의 인콰이어리 또한 확대되고 
있다. HD 한국조선해양은 최근 미국 선급(ABS)로부터 100MW급 규모의 부유식 발
전 설비(BMPP)에 대한 기본 인증(AiP)를 획득했으며, 동사는 FDC 신조 사업과 더불
어 발전원 4 행정 엔진을 파워십 및 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)에 납품하며 그룹사들과 
패키지 사업으로 진행할 예정이다. 가시화된 수요를 기반으로 엔진기계사업부의 구조적 
성장 보여줄 것으로 전망하며, 이에 따른 리레이팅을 예상한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원 유지 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원을 유지한다. 동사는 
올해 상선수주 목표 이미 초과달성하며 안정적인 펀더멘털 유지 중이다. 하반기 남은 
기간은 LNGC, VLGC/VLAC 중심의 선별 수주 전략을 통해 ‘28 년 이후의 상선 부
문 수익성 유지 충분히 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 글로벌 지정학적 리스크 확대에 따른 
해군 현대화 사이클 기조에 맞추어 미 해군은 동맹국의 호위함 구매를 고려하고 있으
며, MASGA를 기반으로 중장기적으로 미국 전투함을 건조하기 위해 HD현대의 미국 
진출 가능성 높다는 판단이다. 또한 미국을 제외한 글로벌 함정(페루, 필리핀, 말레이시
아, 칠레, 사우디, 이집트 등) 수주 파이프라인도 다수 보유하고 있어 함정 모멘텀에 따
른 리레이팅 충분히 가능할 전망이다. 특수선+4 행정 엔진+FDC+SMR 등 조선소에 
붙일 수 있는 모든 추가 성장동력을 보유한 유일한 종목이기에 조선업 최선호주(Top-
Pick)로 제시한다. 최근 불거지고 있는 노조 파업 문제 해소될 시 저점 매수 기회라는 
판단이다.

2026-09-17

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.

당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다.

TUẦN 12-16/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VN-Index có thể hồi kỹ thuật từ cạnh dưới tam giác, song việc chớm thủng hỗ trợ 
1.710 điểm và số mã còn giữ xu hướng tăng từ đáy tháng 7 suy gi ảm khiến xu 
hướng ngắn hạn trở nên trung tính.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

### Page 1

SHIFT IN NARRATIVE

After a rough first half of the year, the 
narrative around Indonesia’s economy has 
turned relatively constructive . The macro 
outlook is on the mend, and policy coherence 
has strengthened. Recent budget 
developments have reduced the likelihood of an 
outright fiscal slippage. A more disciplined 
fiscal trajectory should also provide room for 
monetary policy to stay focused on its price 
stability mandate rather than on ad dressing 
financial stability risks due to fiscal concerns.

On the markets front, e ven as external triggers 
impacted the rupiah, the one -sided sharp 
depreciation run in the currency in the first half 
of the year has stalled . Debt markets have  
attracted offshore interest, alongside strong 
demand for shorter-tenor SRBI securities, all of 
which have contributed to the overall capital 
flows picture.

Ahead of MSCI’s final take on the domestic 
equity market’s classification in November, 
however, equity markets  find little favour . 
Nonetheless, material improvement s in the 
stock listing s and regulatory requirements 
undertaken by the stock market and financial 
regulators are expected to contribute to the 
medium-term stability of the capital markets.

Key domestic risks that plagued the economy 
in the first half of the year have been alleviated 
to some extent, while a few other monitorables 
remain.

www.dbs.com/insightsdirect/macro groupresc@dbs.com 
• Indonesia's macro narrative has 
turned more constructive in 
2H26. 
• Key domestic policy concerns 
have eased. 
• Policy alignment has improved. 
• Greater fiscal discipline should 
allow BI to remain focused on 
price stability.  
• The current account is likely to 
widen as the trade surplus 
narrows. 
• Portfolio and FDI inflows should, 
however, narrow the BOP 
shortfall. 
• Few monitorables remain, 
including factors in the 2027 
fiscal math. 
• IDR performance will remain 
heavily influenced by global 
conditions.

INDONESIA: Policy mix aligns

12 OCTOBER 2026 
Radhika Rao, Senior Economist 
radhikarao@dbs.com

### Page 2

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

NEAR-TERM FISCAL WORRIES 
RECEDE

Fiscal fears have moderated in the near 
term. New Finance Minister Suahasil 
Nazara reaffirmed the government’s 
commitment to maintain the annual 
deficit below -3% of GDP threshold. The 
2026 run-rate suggests that the deficit is 
likely to close within the budgeted target.

By September 2026, the fiscal deficit 
stood at 1.2% of GDP , reflecting a 
normalisation in spending and firm 
revenue growth. Total spending rose by 
a still -strong 1 9% yoy , even though the 
pace moderated from 1H, backed by 
disbursements towards the flagship free 
food program, subsidy and 
compensation outlays , as well as 
personnel expenditure.  Realised 
expenditure stood at 69% of the full -
year budget by 9M26.

On the other hand, revenues rose sharply 
by 25 .5% yoy (partly benefiting from 
base effects as readings were negative 
at the same time last year), helped by 
higher tax collections, export duties, and 
non-tax receipts. Revenues have already 
accounted for 74% of the budgeted 
scale by 9M26, suggesting full -year 
target is likely to be met.

Global crude prices are a key risk for the 
fiscal outlook, as prevailing Brent prices 
are above $100bl at the time of writing 
(vs budgeted ICP at $70bl), which will 
feed through higher subsidy and 
compensation outgoes, as authorities 
remain hesitant to raise fuel pump prices.

Significant efficiency measures have, 
however, been put in place  to contain 
spending, including reductions in 
outlays towards flagship schemes , 
which will help contain the extent of 
deterioration in the fiscal math. For 
instance, the flagship MBG program, 
which started with an allocation of IDR 
35trn, was  moderated to IDR 268trn 
(realised spending by Sep26 was IDR 
153trn). Successive stimulus packages

### Page 3

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

have been announced but the scale has 
been contained.

2027 math signals fiscal prudence, soft 
spots warrant attention

The official goalposts for 2027 point to 
a continued preference for fiscal 
prudence rather than fiscal activism.

Spending is on course to surpass the 
symbolic IDR 4000tn level, ris ing nearly 
4% yoy, with outlays divided between 
the centre and regional governments. 
The mix points to steady allocations 
towards subsidies, social transfers , and 
capital expenditure,  with ministry -level 
spending (K/L) set to contract, while non-
K/L spending is budgeted to increase. 
More notably, spending is expected to 
rise on key flagship programs – for 
instance, social protection (up 10%), the 
largest component education (up 14%) , 
which includes the free meals program 
at IDR 240trn.

On the revenue end, state revenues are 
expected to rise by 9% yoy compared to 
the budgeted 2026 APBN/ 7% vs the 
revised 2026 outlook. Compared to 
baseline 2026, tax revenues are 
expected to rise 10.5%, besides higher 
non-tax collections. This math lands the 
2027 budgeted deficit at -2.4% of GDP 
vs revised 2026 at -2.85% of GDP.

Few uncertainties that loom.  Firstly, 
next year’s math rests on an upbeat 
growth projection of 6 .0% (DBSf 5.2%) , 
besides modest depreciation in the 
rupiah and about  a 7% increase in the 
Indonesia crude price assumption (see 
table). Some of these expectations 
might be challenged as the year unfolds. 
Next, state revenues are expected to 
rise to a n upbeat  12.5% of GDP , likely 
riding on a strong growth forecast, 
better tax due diligence , and improved 
administration via the Coretax 
mechanism.

Thirdly, crude prices are a key risk to the 
fiscal math. Given the economy’s 
position as a net oil importer, various 
official commentaries put the sensitivity  
at every $1pb oil worsening the fiscal 
balance by IDR 6trn, after accounting for 
oil-related revenues. This implies that for 
every $10pb increase, the deficit could 
be impacted by ~IDR 60trn, not far from 
MOF’s sensitivity of IDR 68trn (~0.25 -
0.30% of GDP) due to higher subsidy and 
compensation costs  (ICT assumed at 
$75bl vs prevailing Brent at $100bl). 
Spending will need to reprioritised to 
accommodate additional allocation 
towards subsidies and compensation.

Next, the direction of travel on the 
contribution of SOE dividends from the 
investment agency to the state coffers 
for 2027 remains unclear, after being 
accounted for this year. Lastly, 
successive stimulus measures in-kind 
and wage subsidies were announced this 
year and could marginally add to the 
strain in 2027 (0.1-0.2% of GDP).

Discussions around the State Finance 
Law also warrant a watch. We maintain 
our deficit forecast at a wider -2.8% to -
2.9% range to account for any revenue 
shortfall or a gradual pace of growth. 
Nonetheless, deficits/debt are likely to 
stay below key thresholds  and not 
emerge as a flashpoint for the markets.

MONETARY POLICY ON AN EVEN 
KEEL

Bank Indonesia has left the benchmark 
rate unchanged  since July 2026, after 
100bp hikes in 1H26. While tighter global 
financial conditions and geopolitical 
risks were key risks on the horizon, the BI 
held the domestic GDP forecast at 4.9 -
5.7% (DBSf: 5.3%) alongside 
manageable inflation, notwithstanding 
El Nino-related risks. Inflation rose to 3.3% 
yoy in Sep26, towards the high end of the 
target range. Authorities have, however, 
assured that the impact of higher food 
prices due to poor weather will be 
addressed through administrative 
measures by regional bodies.

Contained inflation expectations ha ve 
reduced the urgency for BI to tighten 
policy further, allowing policymakers to 
prioritise growth and financial stability 
considerations. The emphasis on 
strengthening the balance of payments 
position reflects continued caution over 
external financing conditions and capital 
flows. However, with the rupiah 
expected to remain stable on the back of 
policy measures and fundamenta ls, the 
BI did not view current external 
pressures as severe enough to warrant 
additional tightening.

BI introduced a host of measures at the 
June to September policy meetings , 
including FX monetary hedging 
instruments and a mechanism to provide 
foreign investors with greater flexibility 
in managing IDR-denominated 
investments in Indonesia.  
These included –  
a) swap hedging per tenor bucket was 
increased to 3M - 15%; 6M - 20%; 12M - 
25%, from 12.5% earlier.  
b) DNDF incentive was increased to 6M - 
25% and 12M – 30%. An increase in FX/ 
NDF swap premium incentives for longer 
tenors will lower hedging costs and

### Page 5

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

encourage longer tenor positions, in turn 
increasing longevity of these flows;  
c) Launch of the VASTRA scheme (FX 
Market Transactions for Hedging 
through Partner Banks) got underway, 
with the naming of 14 domestic partner 
banks. This facility is designed to help 
investors transact with lower underlying 
documentation requirements, tap into 
longer banking hours , and access 
onshore hedging facilities.  
d) deepen FX and money markets by 
expanding Local Currency Transactions 
(LCT) between IDR and CNY , besides a 
few other currencies.

Policymakers are likely to tap a 
combination of administrative and 
macroprudential measures rather than 
solely interest rates to defend the 
currency as well as keep inflationary 
expectations in check.

We expect the pause on rates to extend 
into 4Q26.  This call might be at risk if 
depreciation pressures on the rupiah 
mount again on unfavourable global 
developments and if the US Fed 
undertakes a more aggressive hik ing 
cycle than our baseline (of 75bp hikes).

WIDER CAD WILL BE PARTLY 
OFFSET BY POSITIVE FLOWS

Indonesia’s 1H26 current account 
deficit (CAD) widened significantly  to -
$16bn vs -$3bn in the comparable period 
a year ago. Much of this deterioration 
was led by a narrower goods trade 
balance, while  the services deficit 
widened on the seasonal travel account 
due to the Hajj period. Higher coupon 
payments weighed on the primary 
account.  This pressure is likely to persist 
in 2H as the oil & gas trade deficit 
remains wide and export performance 
has been fi rm but tracking lower than 
regional peers, likely also impacted by 
the El Nino phenomenon in key agri 
commodities. We expect full year CAD to 
be closer to 1.8-2% of GDP from -0.1% in 
2025.

Much rests on the capital flows account. 
While 1H balance of payments (BOP) also 
widened, there was palpable 
improvement in 2Q26 vs 1Q.  2Q BOP 
narrowed significantly to $0.9bn from a 
wide $9.1bn in 1Q, helped by positive 
capital account flows – primarily 
stronger portfolio investment on SRBI 
and debt inflows, and net FDI position. 
This dependency is likely to extend to 2H, 
with capital fl ows till Sep26 holding up 
on account of hedging discounts and 
moves to improve inflows. Global 
conditions, including the narrow ID -US 
yields, however, warrant attention. 
Nonetheless, full-year BOP could remain

### Page 6

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

wide, on a scale comparable to the 2025 
gap.

IN SUMMARY

IDR markets have been relatively 
steady in October despite higher oil 
prices and a bid dollar , though 
sentiment remains sensitive to external 
developments and swings in global risk 
appetite. USDIDR traded within 17600 -
18000, while 10Y yield hovered around 
7.15-7.25%. A jump in the Aug goods 
trade surplus aided this respite, 
alongside foreign purchases of domestic 
debt, which have remained positive this 
month and on a  YTD basis. Insurers, the 
central bank, and retail investors have 
remained key anchors for the bond 
market. SRBI rates are off highs to sub -
7% levels but are attracting steady 
demand, likely encouraged by hedging 
discounts. An improved outlook for flows 
has helped contain spillovers from a 
wider current account deficit. This does 
not take away from the fact that the 
rupiah is amongst the worst performers 
on a year-to-date basis.

Separately, markets will also monitor 
developments in the political economy, 
institutional architecture, and formation, 
as well as the role of state -led agencies 
(e.g., DSI) and progress on the 
centralisation of decision -making. We 
expect the BI to keep on hold, however, 
pressure to tighten policy might return if 
the US Fed hikes rates well into 2027 
and the US dollar benefits from a risk-off 
environment, weighing on the currency.

投資焦點美國公債
► 美國財政部 9月10日執行10年期與20年期公
債回購計畫，並擴大回購規模從20億增至60
億美元 ，欲對缺乏 流動性的長天期舊債進行
流動性支持 ，減輕公債拋售 潮引發期限 溢價
走升問題 ，然實際回購規模僅 51.8億美元並
低於預期的 60億至100億美元令市場失望 。
財政部亦公布例行 10年與30年期公債標售結
果，宣布執行擴大回購操作後 ，從投標倍數
與得標 減預期利率 差距來看 ，反映長 天期公
債需求不淡 ，然川普承諾 共和黨若在期中選
舉中獲勝 ，將向全國成年 公民發錢，恐導致
未來公債 供給量續升 ，後續視 美國政府能否

維持財政紀律逐步恢復市場信心。
► 美國非農新增就業數明顯較前月改善 ，反映
就業市 場韌 性 ， 體現 實體 經濟表 現優 於 預
期，然短期 通膨難 回政策目標，預期聯準會
最快9月重啟升息 ，2年期公債利率水準已反
映升息空間 ，考量財政問題 與美國升 息預期
同在 ，投資美國公債宜慎 ，留意Fed官員態
度與經濟預估變化。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 美國經濟未見明顯疲態！為聯準會埋下升息伏筆財政部倍增長期公債回購不如預期，然標售需求回溫
美財政部倍增公債回購不如預期，保守看待公債配置

投資焦點
► 2026年第2季美國標普500企業財報數據顯示
獲利成長明顯高於債務增速 ，另可發現代表
獲利護城河的大型企業毛利率貼近 2022年以
來高位，淨利率亦創1998年以來新高 ，資本
支出年增至 25%歷史高位水準 ，另資本 支出
佔EBITDA比率升至 35%，預期美國大型企
業持續擴大資本 支出推動獲利 成長動能 ，同
時維持獲利高於資本開支的資本效率 ，避免
大額資本支出過度失控 ，造成未來債信體質
與償債能力惡化。
► 從今年1月至8月美國各天期投資級債表現來
看，可以發現中短天期債含息總報酬仍維持
正值，主因美國政府財政 問題與升息擔憂推
升殖利率曲線上行 。從短、中、長天期殖利
率/存續期間比率來看 ，美國短天期債具備利
率波動下最大抗震能力 ，而長天期承受 利率
上行耐受幅度相對僅 50個基本點 ，考量利率
風險可能持續至年底 ，債市投資人可配置中
短天期債券降擾動。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 美國中短天期投資級債年至今表現相對較穩美國大型企業淨利率創1998年來新高

美國大型企業債信仍強，中短天期投資級債為首選

### Page 8

8
股市
 全球主要國家債務攀升 ，利率創高添股市評價修正風險 ，加上科技股舉債投入資本支出 ，未來獲利
與變現能力備受關注 。美伊衝突加劇不確定性 ，短線可透過布局醫療與公用事業等防禦股 ，降低市
場震盪風險 ，科技及AI半導體等發展趨勢不變 ，獲利等基本面支持長線發展 ，採逢回分批布局 ，並
分散國家與產業配置。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率彈升有利銀行股 ，歐、日銀行股基本面佳 ，日本半導體股
分批布局較穩健，歐洲國防軍工仍具投資吸引力。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融、工業等產業機會
區域：日本銀行股、日本半導體股、歐洲
金融、國防
債市
 發債供給與聯準會升息預期 ，美國公債殖利率續升，應避免超長天期債券投資 ，預期年底前美國10
年期公債利率將於4.5%-5.0%震盪，可逢利率走升時 ，以中短天期債券進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體
質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者 ，包含金融、科技、通訊及公

用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會9月升息預期持續，美元短線高檔震盪。
 日本銀行釋出鷹派訊號，9月升息可能性升高，有利日圓止貶回升。
日圓震盪偏升
商品
 中東衝突反覆 ，荷姆茲海峽短期恢復正常通行機率偏低 ，加上石油庫存探低 ，預期今年能源仍呈供
不應求態勢，油價維持偏高水位。
 美國財務壓力與去美元化之兩個長期結構性問題依然存在，央行買盤提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金區間操作，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維

### Page 15

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 344: RBNZ to hike next week as inflation risks mount

Source PDF: all report pdf/RBNZ to hike next week as inflation risks mount.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5

### Page 1

We expect a 25bp hike on 28 October by the Reserve Bank of
New Zealand. Since the dovish September meeting, rising oil
prices, higher front-end rates globally and weakness in the NZ
dollar all point to risks of inﬂation overshooting 4.0% in 4Q. Oﬀ-
meeting commentary has supported market bets on another
hike, and we expect a hawkish-leaning statement
Francesco Pesole
Auckland, New Zealand

### Page 2

In September, the Reserve Bank of New Zealand raised rates to
2.75% but pushed back against expectations for a large tightening
cycle. Its projections pointed to the next hike only in December or
1Q27 and a 3.25% peak in 2028-2029.
The picture has turned increasingly hawkish since then. We now
expect a hike on 28 October, in line with market pricing,
accompanied by a hawkish statement. We expect another hike in
either December or February, with a slight preference for February,

and see chances of another move later in 2027. Our terminal rate
estimate is 3.25-3.5%.
Markets input has been hawkish for the
RBNZ
Source: ING, Macrobond
A lot has changed since September
Third-quarter inﬂation slowed from 4.1% to 3.7%, below both the
RBNZ’s 3.9% estimate and our 3.8% forecast. Non-tradable CPI, a
measure of core inﬂation, came in at 3.3% versus the RBNZ’s 3.6%
estimate. In isolation, those numbers would have reinforced
September’s dovish message and argued for an October hold.

Other developments, however, have argued for earlier action:
1. Higher energy prices. The RBNZ’s September projections
assumed oil prices would gradually decline. Instead, they have
risen around 20% since the September meeting. That has
materially increased the chances of 4Q inﬂation exceeding

4.0%, compared with the RBNZ’s 3.9% estimate, and remaining
stickier afterwards.
2. Weaker Kiwi dollar. The trade-weighted value of NZD tumbled
4% in September, and is at the lowest since 2009 (chart below).
The RBNZ has been one of the culprits behind a weak NZD, and
while it doesn’t normally react to FX moves, the depreciation is
increasing imported inﬂation risk.
3. Resilient growth. Second-quarter GDP surprised to the upside
at 2.6% YoY, against the RBNZ’s 2.1% estimate. Activity surveys

suggest growth may remain around 2.0% in both 3Q and 4Q,
leaving the RBNZ more room to ﬁght inﬂation with higher rates.
4. Hawkish repricing elsewhere. US two-year swap rates have
risen around 35bp since the start of September, with NZD 2yr
swaps following 33bp higher. Australia’s increase was smaller
at 15bp, but so is the starting point for rates. The RBA raised
rates again to 4.6% in September and kept the door open for
more.

October move

helped cement market expectations for an October hike despite
softer 3Q inﬂation. He described this month’s meeting as “wide
open”, contrary to what the rate projections had suggested,

acknowledged market pricing and highlighted upside risks to the
3.9% 4Q inﬂation projection from higher oil prices and NZD
depreciation.
The focus on 4Q inﬂation seemed to us to be a signal for markets
to look past the softer 3Q data. Governor Anna Breman reinforced
that message today, highlighting concerns about a higher 4Q
ﬁgure and eﬀectively neutralising any dovish impact from the
release. Markets now price in 21bp for this meeting. The proximity

of New Zealand’s 7 November general election does not seem a
suﬃcient reason for the RBNZ to deliver a big dovish surprise by
holding rates.
Hawkish hike to help NZD
We see little reason for the RBNZ to hike and pair the move with
dovish signals. Renewed focus on the currency means its
communication may be more market-aware than usual, while
resilient growth allows it to err on the hawkish side.
There are no new projections at this meeting, giving the RBNZ more

ﬂexibility to adjust its policy message through the statement
without sounding committal on the rate path. We think the
message will focus on a) keeping the door open to a December
hike; and b) signalling that the hiking cycle can be longer and faster
than previously estimated. We expect this to validate market
pricing for around 75bp of tightening by end-1Q27.
We saw good upside potential for NZD in our latest monthly FX
update, as the currency looked cheap by short-term valuation

metrics and there was room for further hawkish repricing in NZD
front-end rates. Our 0.570 one-month target has already been
reached, and we admit further NZD/USD gains depend more on
global/US developments than domestic factors. Still, the pair is
roughly 1% undervalued in the short term, and some emerging
USD idiosyncratic weakness around AI concerns, combined with a
hawkish RBNZ hike, can help build a stronger ﬂoor and leave
potential for further exploration.

Standard Chartered Bank

Charts of the week: No capitulation

A hotter-than-expected US inflation report triggered a pullback in risk assets, but there are few signs of capitulation so far 
US core and supercore inflation; US inflation breadth*    Our ‘Fear and Greed’ indicator

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *share of US consumer inflation components rising more than 3%

Editorial 
Staying defensive and diversified  
Strategy summary:  Two weeks ago, we turned defensive 
towards equities, locking in semiconductor equity gains amid 
elevated bond volatility, narrowing market breadth and political 
uncertainty before the US mid -term elections. The near -term 
outlook reflected a less favourable risk-reward balance. Since 
we turned defensive , the Nasdaq 100 index has recorded its 
biggest weekly decline in three months as September’s hotter-
than-expected US inflation report briefly lifted bond yields. 
We have extended our defensive stance this week, 
downgrading US Communication Services equities to Neutral 
and upgrading US Healthcare to Overweight. We also closed 
our opportunistic bullish Communication Services idea and 
initiated a bullish idea on Healthcare, where earnings visibility 
and defensive characteristics appear more attractive. In fixed 
income, we see opportunities in US 10-year government bonds. 
We await clearer AI profit margins and spending guidance, 
broader earnings growth or lower bond and equity market 
volatility before turning positive on equities. Until then, 
diversification and disciplined entry levels remain important. 
Hot US inflation triggers equity pullback; not hot enough 
for an October Fed hike:  Risk assets pulled back after 
September’s hotter-than-expected inflation data (headline 3.7% 
y/y; core 2.6% y/y). Higher gasoline and diesel prices were the 
main drivers, but passthrough appeared in airline fares, used 
cars and transportation services, showing pressures were not 
confined to energy. Almost half of inflation components are still 
rising above 3%, a measure recently cited by Fed Chair Warsh. 
Producer inflation also beat estimates. The reports revive the 
possibility of an October Fed rate hike, but we expect a divided 
Fed to weigh it against September’s soft jobs report and 
narrowly hold this month. A December hike appears more likely 
if inflation remains firm . Although Fed Chair Warsh did not 
comment on policy at an IMF speech this week, New York Fed 
chief Williams said he saw no need to hike in 2027. Our base 
case remains two further hikes by June 2027. 
Limited equity pullback so far.  Equities fell sharply after the 
US inflation report, but the pullback is limited. The Nasdaq 100 
is 4.5% below its record high, while the S&P 500 remains close 
to all -time highs despite the latest decline. The S&P 500 
volatility index (VIX) has risen from below 15 to 1 8.2, still well 
under the 25 –30 range typically associated with capitulation 
and forced selling. Meanwhile, US government bond volatility 
(MOVE) remains elevated after surging since September.  Our 
stock-bond quant model turned more negativ e (-2) this week, 
signalling elevated equity volatility in the near term.  
When to turn more constructive on stocks?  We would turn 
more constructive when capitulation signals align: the Nasdaq 
100 approaches a 10% drawdown, the VIX rises to 25 –30 and 
our ‘Fear and Greed’ index  enters “extreme fear”. Greater 
confidence would also require easing concerns about AI 
spending and technology firms’ exposure to neocloud revenue, 
lower bond volatility and our stock-bond model turning positive 
again. Until these conditions emerge, volatility may remain 
elevated, favouring a defensive equity stance. Political risk 
should ease after the US mid -term elections. We continue to 
seek selective opportunities following excessive corrections. 
US financials remain a preferred sector after Q3 earnings:  
US large banks’ earnings were mixed, but underlying trends 
remain encouraging. Healthy loan and deposit growth supports 
net interest income, while resilient consumer spending, wealth 
management activity and a solid M&A and refinancing pipeline 
strengthen the outlook. Credit risks appear manageable . We 
remain constructive and favour stronger, diversified franchises. 
US government bonds offer value as yields spike:  The 
reversal in US government bond yields after their initial inflation-
driven spike suggests limited further upside. The 10 -year yield 
briefly reached 5.32% before slumping as falling equities 
prompted short-covering. We expect a near -term 5.00–5.25% 
range and view moves above 5.25% as attractive entry points 
for our opportunistic 10-year government bond idea. Structural 
pressures, including term premium and increasing supply to 
fund fiscal deficits and AI investments, prevent us from turning 
outright bullish on longer maturities. We, therefore, continue to 
prefer 3–7-year maturities in broader bond allocations.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions 
  How does the September 2026 US Consumer Price Index 
(CPI) report alter your view on the USD and bond yields?   
Our view: The hotter-than-expected CPI print raises the odds of 
an October Fed hike, though we still expect a hold followed by 
a rate hike in December. In the near term, we maintain our view 
that the US 10 -year government bond yield will range between 
5.00% and 5.25%, while  the USD will remain supported as 
markets reassess the likelihood of further Fed hikes. Additional 
gains would depend partly on markets pricing a faster or more 
prolonged tightening cycle, while clearer signs of an 
approaching policy rate peak could see the USD ease back. 
Rationale: September US consumer inflation  came in above 
consensus across the board – headline CPI rose 3.7% y/y (vs. 3.6% 
estimate), and core CPI rose 2.6% y/y (vs. 2.5% estimate) – marking 
the fastest headline acceleration in five months. This shifts the 
balance of risks modestly hawkish . Unsurprisingly, the market -
implied probability of an October Fed hike rose over 50% overnight 
but closed the day at 32.7% – 9ppt higher than Tuesday’s close. 
We believe a split Fed will weigh this inflation report against the soft 
September jobs report and narrowly vote to keep rates on hold this 
month. Our base case remains for two more Fed hikes  by June 
2027. A December rate hike  now looks more like a done deal, 
especially as persistently higher oil prices keep headline and core 
inflation elevated for at least the next few months. Any re-escalation 
of the Middle East conflict after the  US mid-term elections would 
exert pressure on the Fed, although we believe Republicans would  
show greater restraint in US foreign action if they suffer steep Senate 
losses, which looks increasingly likely. 
The intra-day price action in US government bonds was instructive. 
The US 10-year yield briefly spiked to 5.32% on the print before 
closing materially lower, as pre -existing bearish positioning was 
unwound and Fed speakers (Barkin, Goolsbee) stopped well short 
of signalling an imminent move. This dynamic reinforces our view 
that the technical selling pressure driving yields towards the upper 
end of our range is fading. Structural drivers – term premium, fiscal 
supply concerns and ‘higher for longer’ Fed repricing – remain intact 
and keep us from turning outright bullish on duration. However, our 
opportunistic bullish idea in US 10-year government bonds initiated 
at September-end remains, as we continue to view spikes in US 10-
year yields over 5.25% as a good risk-reward entry. From a longer-
term portfolio standpoint, we continue to favour the 3-7-year tenor. 
On FX, elevated US government bond yields should sustain the 
USD’s interest rate appeal. However, expected hikes alone will not 
drive further appreciation. Stronger data or firmer Fed guidance 
would provide fresh support, while evidence that tightening is 
nearing completion would temper the USD’s strength. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

elevated US government bond yields

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are your key takeaways from US bank earnings? 
Our view: Q3 2026 earnings results from major US banks have 
been mixed, but underlying trends remain encouraging. Healthy 
loan and deposit growth, resilient consumer spending and 
wealth management activity continue to support the sector. 
Credit risks remain manageable. We remain constructive on the 
US financial sector, favouring stronger, diversified franchises. 
Rationale: Healthy loan and deposit growth continue to support net 
interest income (NII), hinting that the US economy remains resilient. 
Capital markets revenue grew year -on-year (y/y) , but moderated 
sequentially after a very strong Q2 2026, although performance 
varied across banks. Management commentary points to a healthy 
deal pipeline , supported by M&A activity and debt refinancing 
demand, although execution will depend on market conditions. 
Provisioning and net charge -off trends were mixed across banks, 
with no clear signs of broad deterioration in credit quality. Firmer 
US inflation raises the risk of further monetary tightening. While 
higher rates can support asset yields, they can also increase 
funding costs, pressure borrowers and weigh on securities ’ 
valuations. We will continue to monitor lending demand, credit 
costs, NII trends, capital markets momentum and capital returns.  
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Can Big Tech’s earnings guidance sustain confidence in 
the AI investment cycle? 
Our view: We expect Big Tech’s AI spending to remain elevated, 
supporting our constructive AI capex outlook. However, we 
await guidance on the pace of capex growth and the 
sustainability of profit margins. 
Rationale: Semiconductor leaders’ latest company guidance points 
to supply constraints extending through 2028, supporting continued 
capacity expansion for compute . However, while AI capex is still 
likely to rise, y/y growth is expected to moderate  from c.100% in 
Q3 2026 to c.20% in Q3 2027. This normalisation in growth could 
raise concerns about the AI investment cycle’s durability.  
The Nasdaq-100 Index has rallied alongside accelerating AI capex. 
Slower capex growth is likely to moderate semiconductor revenue 
growth, while hyperscalers continue to incur depreciation costs on 
past investments. The slower growth is already partially reflected in 
the Nasdaq -100’s valuation de -rating this year. While we remain 
constructive on the AI investment theme, we prefer to stay 
prudent in the near term and await Big Tech’s guidance for greater 
visibility into spending plans in H2 2027 and beyond. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

US banks’ revenues remain resilient despite 
moderation from a record-high Q2 2026

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would France face a credit rating downgrade by rating 
agencies this month? What might be the market implications? 
Our view: A rating downgrade is unlikely unless France’s 2027 
budget passes the National Assembly very soon with material 
changes to the proposal. We see French government bond 
yields remaining elevated relative to German Bund yields. 
Rationale: The French National Assembly began debating France’s 
2027 draft budget on 13 October 2026, with the first vote scheduled 
for 20 October. Near-term budget approval appears unlikely, as 
opposition parties have tabled motions to reject the proposal. The 
French Parliament has up to 70 days from the budget’s submission 
to complete the legislative process, although  its final adoption and 
the resolution of political challenges could extend into early 2027.  
Last October, two of the three major rating agencies downgraded 
France by one notch to the high-single-A category  with stable 
outlooks. The remaining agenc y rated France in the low-AA 
category with a negative outlook. As negative outlooks are typically 
resolved within 12 -18 months, agenc ies have time to await fiscal 
clarity before acting. We believe downgrade risks are largely priced 
in after the recent rise in yields, but volatility is likely to persist amid 
fiscal uncertainty and the approaching presidential election. Banks 
and domestically-focused issuers would face the greatest near-term 
pressure. However, we would view wider spreads as an opportunity 
to add exposure, particularly to French banks’ subordinated 
debt, given their stronger balance sheets and the fact that France 
contributes less than 50% of major French banks’ overall revenue. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Japanese banks? 
Our view: We are positive on Japanese banks despite the recent 
market correction, as the structural drivers of improving 
profitability and shareholder returns remain intact . While 
political risk and shifting BoJ rate hike odds could sustain near-
term volatility, we see the recent pullback as an opportunity to 
build selective exposure to Japanese bank stocks. 
Rationale: Japan’s gradual monetary policy normalisation  
should support banks’ NII as floating-rate loans reprice and maturing 
assets are reinvested at higher yields. Healthy corporate lending 
demand, improving capital efficiency and continued share buybacks 
provide further support . While uncertainty over the pace of BoJ 
tightening could weigh on sentiment, we see gradual interest rate 
normalisation as supportive of medium-term bank profitability.  
Key risks  include rising deposit costs, mark -to-market losses on 
bond portfolios and a disorderly increase in Japanese government 
bond yields. We continue to favour well-capitalised banks  with 
strong domestic deposit franchises and improving returns on equity. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Could NZD/USD rebound from its one-year trough? If so, 
how far could it recover?  
Our view: New Zealand’s improving domestic conditions, 
persistent inflation and technical support provide a basis for 
NZD/USD’s initial recovery towards the 0.57 level, although 
elevated US bond yields could slow its progress. 
Rationale: In Q3, New Zealand’s  seasonally-adjusted business 
confidence reading rose to a net 40% of firms expecting the economy 
to improve, up from net 14% in Q2 . Hiring intentions improved, and 
filled jobs rose 0.2% m/m in August. These developments suggest 
domestic conditions remain supportive for the NZD.  
New Zealand’s above-target inflation keeps further RBNZ tightening 
in focus. The 22 October consumer inflation data release and the 
28 October RBNZ policy meeting are key catalysts: persistent price 
pressures a nd improving demand are expected to prompt firmer 
policy guidance and strengthen the NZD’s interest-rate appeal. 
NZD/USD’s position near the lower boundary of its downtrend 
channel suggests scope for a technical rebound. We see 0.57 as 
the first recovery hurdle; a sustained break  above this level  would 
strengthen the case for further gains. Firmer US  consumer inflation 
remains a headwind, making a gradual recovery more plausible 
than a sharp reversal.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  Can the proposed Russia -Ukraine ‘energy ceasefire ’ 
contain oil prices as Middle East risks rise? 
Our view: We expect escalating Middle East tensions to keep oil 
prices elevated . We  maintain our  three- and 12 -month West 
Texas Intermediate (WTI) oil price forecasts at USD 100/bbl and 
USD 80/bbl, respectively, and continue to favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a portfolio hedge 
against persistent inflation risks. 
Rationale: The proposed Russia-Ukraine ‘energy ceasefire’ still 
looks some way off. In our view, even if an agreement is reached, it 
would do little to ease the supply risks building in the Middle East. 
Gulf oil exports have recovered to above 80% of pre-conflict levels 
in recent weeks, helping moderate the pace of price increases. 
However, the recovery remains vulnerable to disruptions, particularly 
following the latest Houthi attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure . 
Diesel and jet fuel remain under the most pressure , with Gulf 
exports of these fuels in September  at only around 60% of pre -
conflict levels. A sustained disruption to major Saudi production or 
export infrastructure remains the principal upside risk to oil prices. 
—  Anthony Naab, Investment Strategist

中国
◼ 製造業PMIは拡大ペースが加速
９月のRatingDog製造業PMIは52.1と、
前月（51.5）から拡大ペースが加速。世
界的なハイテク・AIインフラ需要を背景
に電子機器などの新規受注が堅調に推移
した。
◼ インフレ率はわずかに加速
９月の総合CPIは前年比＋0.9%と、前月
（同＋0.8%）からわずかに加速したも
のの、依然として低水準で推移。輸送費
などが上昇した一方、豚肉価格を中心に
食料価格が引き続き下落し、総合CPIの
上昇を抑えた。
◼ 消費は低位で推移
８月の小売売上高は前年比＋0.4%にと
どまり、消費の回復ペースが鈍化。８月
の不動産販売面積は同▲13.0％と、依然
として減少が続いており、不動産市況の
低迷が長期化している。政府はリスク抑
制のため未完成物件の事前販売規制を強
化を打ち出している。
◼ 輸出は増加幅が拡大
９月の輸出は前年比+36.3%と、前月
（同+25.0%）から増加幅が拡大。世界
的なAI投資の拡大を背景に、半導体など
のハイテク製品や機械電気製品の出荷が

引き続き増加した。

ビス税）減税発効前の買い控えがあっ
たベース効果に加え、農村部の需要が
回復したことで増加幅が拡大した。

の減速を補い、全体を大きく押し上げ
た。

の新規受注が増加し、景況感が改善し
た。
◼ インフレは率は幾分鈍化
９月の総合CPIは前年比＋2.9%と、前
月（同＋3.1 %）から幾分鈍化。引き続
き輸送費が押し上げ要因となったもの
の、前月の携帯電話料金引き下げの裏
要因が剥落し、総合CPIを押し下げた。
◼ 消費は前月比で減少
８月の小売売上高は前期比▲1.8％と、
前月（同▲2.4％）に続き減少。猛暑や
物価、高金利上昇による実質可処分所
得の圧迫を受け、前月比で減少基調が
継続。高額耐久財や衣服などの裁量的
消費において家計の節約志向が意識さ
れている。
◼ 輸出は引き続き増加
９月の輸出は前年比＋83.7%と引き続
き増加。世界的なAI設備投資の拡大に
より、半導体輸出が同＋262.8%と、
前月（同＋209.1 %）に続き驚異的な伸
びを示し、輸出全体を牽引した。

◼ PMIは引き続き縮小
９月の総合PMIは49.2と、前月
（49.5）から縮小ペースが加速。地政
学的リスクに伴う物流コストの上昇や、
中国本土・域内の需要鈍化が民間企業
の景況感を下押しした。
◼ インフレ率は横ばいで推移
８月の総合CPIは前年比＋1.7％と、前
月（同＋1.7％）から横ばいで推移。
◼ 消費は緩やかに増加
８月の実質小売売上高は前年比＋
2.8％と、前月（同＋2.4％）から緩や
かに増加。週末を中心に香港住民が深
センなど中国本土へ越境して買い物を
行う「北上消費」の定着により、域内
の百貨店や外食チェーンの売上が伸び
悩んだ。

８月の輸出量は前年比＋28.6％と、前
月（同＋31.7％）に続き増加。中国本
土を経由する先端半導体およびAI関連
ハイテク部材の荷動きが活発だったこ
とにより、前年比で底堅いプラス成長
を維持した。

◼ PMIは縮小に転じる

界的な半導体・電子部品サイクルの需
要を背景に、主力の集積回路などの出
荷が大幅に増加した。

したものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所が
信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、
完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告なく変更されることが
あります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社
の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限りません。

Document 447: イベントカレンダー『各国の主要政治・経済イベント予定』(2026年11月号)

Source PDF: all report pdf/イベントカレンダー『各国の主要政治・経済イベント予定』(2026年11月号).pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 1
Status: ok

### Page 1

各国の主要政治 ・ 経済イベン ト予定 （10月 ７ 日時点）
11月の各国主要政治 ・ 経済イベン ト
日本 米国 グローバル
上旬
中間選挙 （3日）
10月ISM製造業景況指数 （3日）

10月小売売上高 （17日) (欧） 7-9月期GDP改定値 （13日）
下旬
10月消費者物価指数 （20日）
10月鉱工業指数 （30日）
7-9月期GDP改定値 （25日）
（注）  作成時点で、 第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所経済調査部が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、 その正確性、 完全性に対す
る責任は負いません。
米国中間選挙は、 大統領任期4年の中間である2年目に行われる連邦議会選挙です。 2026年11月3日に実施され、 下

院の全435議席と上院の約3分の1が改選対象となります。 政権に対する 「中間評価」 と しての意味合いを持ち、 今後の
政策運営に大きな影響を与えます。
本選に先立って行われた各党の候補者選び （予備選挙） では、 トランプ大統領の後押しを受けた共和党候補が相次い
で勝ち上がった一方、 民主党でも現職政権への対決姿勢を鮮明にする候補が選ばれるなど、 両党ともに熱心な支持層
の意向が強く出た結果となりました。 しかし本選に向けては、 高止まりする物価や生活費負担への不満を背景に トランプ
大統領の支持率が低下傾向にあると報じ られるなど、 政権側には逆風も吹いています。 有権者の間では、 長引く中東情
勢の悪化を受けたインフレや雇用環境といった 「生活防衛」 が最大の投票判断基準となっているほか、 国境管理 ・ 不法移
民問題や人工妊娠中絶の権利といった社会的テーマも、 勝敗の鍵を握る激戦区 （スイング ・ ステー ト） での浮動票の行方
を左右する重要な争点です。
執筆時点の情勢と しては、 上院は共和党優位、 下院は接戦ながら民主党がやや有利とみられています。 先行きへの影

響と しては、 共和党が上下両院の過半数を維持する 「 ト リプルレ ッ ド」 が続けば、 トランプ政権の政策実行力が高まり規制
緩和などが進みやすくなる一方、 関税引き上げや財政悪化への懸念が残ります。 反対に、 民主党が下院を制して 「ねじれ
議会」 となった場合は、 法案や予算が通りにく くなり政権運営が停滞する反面、 過度な強硬策にブレーキがかかる点がか
えって安心材料と受け止められる側面もあります。 中間選挙の結果は、 米国の国内政治にとどまらず、 今後の通商政策
や金利の先行きを見極める分岐点となります。 （主席エコノミス ト ：  阿原健一郎）
11月の政治 ・ 経済イベン ト 「米国中間選挙」
4第一ライフ研レポー ト 2026.11

１．不動産販売床面積 
・中国国家統計局が 2026年 10月 19日に 26年 9月の不動産関連統計を発表した。不動産販売床面
積の前年同月比（当社試算値、図表 1）は、▲20.7％となった（前月は▲14.8％）。当社試算の季節
調整済み前月比（図表 2）は、▲5.6％となった（前月は▲2.1％）。

２．新築住宅販売価格（70都市） 
・26年 9月の新築住宅販売価格 （70都市単純平均）は、前年同月比で▲3.0％となった （前月は▲3.3％）
（図表 3）。1～3線都市で下落幅が縮小した。前月比では、▲0.14％となった（前月は▲0.17％）
（図表 4）。1線都市の上昇幅は横ばいで推移し、2線都市では下落幅が縮小した。

５．その他指標 
・不動産開発資金の前年同月比（当社試算値、図表 8）は、▲26.4％となった（前月は▲26.7％）。 
・完成在庫床面積は 7.48億㎡で、前年同月比のマイナス幅は前月から拡大した（図表 9） 。住宅着工
床面積（3カ月後方移動平均、以下同）は前年同月比のマイナス幅が横ばいで推移、住宅竣工床面
積の前年同月比はマイナス幅が拡大した。

1 不動産開発投資を基準指標とし、不動産投資・資金・面積・販売関連の指標を選び、季節要因を取り除いて算出した
指標。通常、100が最適、95～105が適度、95以下が不景気、105以上が過熱気味。

### Page 3

中国
(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年9月2
景気は内需主因で停滞
◆消費・投資はコロナ禍以来の低い伸び
中国景気は内需の不振で足踏み 。小売売上高
は、財消費の低迷を主因に前年並みの水準で推移。
耐久消費財の買い替え補助金の効果がはく落して
いるほか、中東危機によるガソリン価格の高騰などを
背景に消費者マインドが一段と悪化 。固定資産投
資はインフラ投資などで底打ちの兆しがみられるもの
の、昨年以降大幅な減少が継続。
一方、外需は高い伸びを維持 。世界的なAIブー
ムを背景にIT関連財の輸出が引き続き好調である
ほか、EV需要の高まりを受けて自動車輸出台数も
3カ月連続で過去最高水準。もっとも、原油や半導
体などの価格上昇の影響も大きく 、実質ベースでは、
内需の減速を補うには至らず。
◆成長率目標の達成は困難
先行きの景気も、内需を中心に停滞が続く見通し。
ハイテク製造業の投資拡大や電力 ・通信網などの
「６つのネットワーク 」推進によるインフラ投資の持ち
直しが見込まれるものの 、不動産不況や過剰生産

能力といった構造的な下押し要因が残存し 、投資
は総じて低調に推移する見込み。悲観的な消費者
マインドが続くなか、消費も低迷が続く公算大。
一方、政府は景気刺激策に慎重 。成長率よりも
経済の質を重視し 、不動産市場や地方政府債務
などのリスク解消に注力する姿勢を明確にしており 、
2026年の成長率目標は達成困難な状況。
経済政策スタンス（鐘才文コラム）
（資料）人民網などを基に日本総研作成
（注）中央財経委員会が「鐘才文」名義で掲載した文章。
現状認識
・AI、ロボット、ハイテク製造業など新たな成長ドラ
イバーが台頭
・成長モデルの転換期にあり、成長率や月次指標
よりも経済運営の質を重視
・供給は強い一方、需要が弱い状況は残存
経済運営の
方向性
・AIや先端製造業を軸とした産業高度化を推進
・大規模な景気刺激策には依存しない
・不動産市場や地方政府債務などのリスク解消策
に注力（短期の成長率にはマイナスとなるも、長
期かつ持続的な経済発展にはプラス）
下半期の
経済見通し
・第15次５カ年計画などで確定した重点プロジェ

クトを執行すれば、経済は安定的に推移

(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年9月3
消費は総じて低調
◆サービス消費は堅調も、財消費が低迷
８月の小売売上高は前年比＋ 0.3％と、ほぼゼ
ロ成長。財消費の低迷が主因であり、なかでも自動
車が大幅減。買い替え補助政策で喚起された需要
の反動減に加え、原油高でガソリン車需要が減退。
宝飾品も 、国際的な金価格の上昇と節約志向の
高まりが相まって、販売数量が大幅減。
先行きの消費も、総じて低調に推移する見通し。
消費者マインドは中東危機を契機に悪化基調 。６
月に一時持ち直したものの 、ガソリン価格の再上昇
を受けて再び低下 。政府は利子補給制度の対象
拡大などで内需を喚起しているものの 、大規模な消
費刺激策は打ち出さない姿勢。一方、サービス消費
は今後の消費拡大の重点分野に位置づけられてお
り、堅調に推移する見込み。
◆住宅市場は長期停滞から脱却できず
住宅市場は引き続き低迷。大都市を中心に住宅
購入要件の緩和などが実施されているものの 、購入
層の減少といった構造的な要因が住宅販売の重石

に。新築住宅価格に加え 、市場の実勢を反映しや
すい中古住宅価格も引き続き下落。在庫の回転期
間も長期化している模様。住宅市場の健全化に向
け、予約販売から完成後販売への移行や住宅ロー
ン返済期間の延長などが打ち出されたものの 、効果
が短期間で現れるとは見込めず 、住宅市場の本格
回復にはなお数年を要する見通し。
住宅販売に関する新たな政策
（資料）国家金融監督管理総局などを基に日本総研作成

### Page 5

中国
(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年9月4
◆投資は下げ止まりの兆し
８月の固定資産投資は前年比 ▲10.6％と、大
幅減が続いているものの、一部で底打ちの兆し。セク
ター別にみると 、インフラ投資は 、「６つのネットワー
ク」の推進などを背景にマイナス幅が縮小。一方、製
造業投資は減少幅が拡大 。政府は経済運営の質
を重視する姿勢をとっており、過剰生産能力の解消
が投資の重石になっている可能性。不動産投資も、
住宅市場の低迷を背景に大幅減少が継続。
◆年末にかけてマイナス幅が縮小
先行きの投資は、以下２点から減少幅が縮小す
る見通し。第１に、ハイテク製造業の投資拡大。政
府による支援も相まって、半導体やロボットなど先端
分野は増産基調。第２に、インフラ投資の持ち直し。
8,000億元の新型政策性金融ツールと「６つのネッ
トワーク（六張網）」が年後半のインフラ投資を押し
上げ。六張網は官民が重点的にインフラ整備を進め
る戦略分野であり 、既存整備計画の前倒しのみな
らず、次世代通信網や計算能力網などの新規計画

も立ち上げ。足元では、情報伝送や鉄道輸送 、航
空輸送に関連する固定資産投資に底打ちの兆し。
もっとも、本格回復は見込めない状況 。製造業や
不動産業では、過剰な生産能力や在庫などの構造
的な下押し要因が残存 。インフラ投資も量的拡大
は限定的。財源となる地方債は借り換えや隠れ債
務の圧縮が優先され、新発債の発行は停滞。
投資は下げ止まるも、弱い動き

### Page 6

中国
(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年9月5
外需は先行きも堅調
◆輸出入ともに価格上昇の影響大
８月の名目輸出は前年比＋ 24.9％と、高い伸
び。ASEAN・NIEs向けでは、IT関連財の輸出が引
き続き好調であるほか 、中東危機による原油供給
懸念を背景にEV輸出も拡大。加えて、対米輸出が
米中関係の改善を受けて堅調に推移しているほか、
EU向け輸出も、年初ほどの勢いはないものの底堅く
推移。こうした輸出額の増加には 、価格上昇の影
響も大。原油価格が高止まりしているほか 、AIブー
ムを背景に半導体や銅 、金属鉱石などの価格も上
昇。実質ベースの輸出の伸びは弱く、内需の減速を
補うには至らず。
先行きも、外需は堅調に推移する見通し。９月の
米中首脳会談でも貿易摩擦の緩和に向けた認識
で一致しており、対米輸出は前年比プラスで推移す
る見通し。また、中東危機を受けて世界的な脱炭
素化が一段と加速しており 、EV輸出が拡大する公
算大。ASEANなどへの中間財輸出も 、AIブームを

背景に好調を維持する見込み。
◆資金流出は拡大傾向
金融収支は流出傾向が継続 。内需の不振で対
内直接投資が減少しているほか 、準備資産も縮小。
商業銀行などへの外貨資金の振替・預託や資産再
配分が進行している可能性 。先行きも、内需の低
迷が対内投資の重石となり 、金融収支でみた資本
流出傾向が継続する見通し。

### Page 7

中国
(株)日本総合研究所 中国経済展望 2026年9月6
◆根強いディスインフレ圧力
８月の生産者物価（PPI）は前年比＋3.8％と、
高水準で推移。原油価格に起因する生産財価格
の高止まりが主因 。消費者物価（CPI）も、総合
ベースで同＋0.8％、コアベースで同＋1.0％と、前
月からやや加速。交通燃料のプラス幅が再び拡大し
たほか、半導体の価格高騰で通信機器が上昇 。
もっとも、インフレ率は政府目標の２ ％前後を大きく
下回っており、内需の停滞に起因するディスインフレ
圧力が強い状況。
先行きも、エネルギー価格の高止まりや半導体価
格の上昇を背景に一部財の価格上昇圧力は根強
いものの 、内需の低迷により基調的なインフレ率は
低位で推移する見込み。
◆対米ドルレートは増価基調
９月の人民元の対米ドルレートは増価基調で推
移。中東危機が経済に与える影響や金融政策の
動向を見極めようと、市場の様子見姿勢が続くもの
の、好調な輸出や資本流出の抑制に向けた人民銀
行の元高容認姿勢もあり、小幅な人民元高圧力が

続く見通し。
◆株価は下落基調
９月の上海総合株価指数は下落。PPIの上昇率
が一服したことを好感して持ち直す動きがみられるも
のの、中東情勢の不透明感や内需の弱さが相場の
重石に。景気が停滞局面に入る なか、先行きも弱
い動きが続く見込み。

### Page 9

日本総合研究所主催 シンポジウムのご案内
米中対立の常態化や米国の自国第一主義の強まりを背景に、既存の国際経済秩序が揺らいでいます。こうしたなか、各国は存
在感を高めるグローバルサウス諸国との外交・経済関係の強化を急いでいます。日本も、OECD（経済協力開発機構）などと協調し
ながら、関係強化を目指していますが、現状はASEANやインドなどに偏重しており、アフリカや中南米との連携は限定的です。本シン
ポジウムでは、はじめに世界経済の構造変化や日本のグローバルサウス諸国との連携策の現状・課題を多角的に検証します。その
上で、日本がグローバルサウス諸国との関係を深化させるための具体的な戦略を議論します。

3Q/4Q26 EPS 下修 5/14%，主因產能不足+ 歐洲市場待復甦 
上銀 3Q26/4Q26 營收估 81.1/81.3 億元，季增 8%/季持平，下修 5/14%，主
因公司人力產能提升不足且新招工學習曲線須時間提升。整體 2H26 將持續
受惠中國十五五規劃帶動工具機產業升級、 AI 資料中心與機器人帶動螺桿 /
線軌等自動化零組件需求成長。歐洲市場需求尚未顯著成長，(過往擴張時期
PMI 達 55-65，仍高於 9M26 52.9) ，估使公司於短期產能擴張速度較過去上
升循環為保守。毛利率估 36.0/35.3%，季增 1.8/季減 0.7 個 ppts，下修 0.1
個 ppts/維持前次預估。EPS 估達 3.1/3.0 元，下修 5/14%。 
短期受產能不足壓抑出貨動能，長期看好中/歐需求回升 
儘管短期受人力瓶頸與歐洲市場 處復甦初期而壓抑出貨動能，然長線公司將
受惠 1) 歐洲市場復甦（高毛利歐洲營收占公司營收 25–28%）; 2) 半導體設
備放量; 3) 訂單能見度拉長伴隨產業漲價挹注毛利率。考量產能提升受限與
歐洲市場復甦力道略低於預期 ，估上銀 2026/27 年營收年增 24%/16%至
301.2/348.6 億元，下修 6%/10%；毛利率 34.5%/35.0%，年增 5.7/0.5 個
ppts，下修 0.1/0.1 個 ppts；營益率 14.6%/17.7%，下修 0.2/0.1 個 ppts；估
EPS 達 10.1/13.5 元，年增 133%/34%，下修 6%/10%。 
AI 驅動產業續強，然短期待歐洲市場成長帶動第二波漲價 
本中心認為上銀短期因產線人力瓶頸壓抑產能擴充速度，且整體需求仍待歐
洲市場強勁復甦以帶動第二波產業漲價。 然公司積極布局 AI/半導體/人形機
器人等核心關鍵零組件，將持續優化產品組合。上銀歷史本益比區間為 20–
76 倍（五年均值 34 倍）；考量產能提升受阻、歐洲顯著復甦時程後移及全
球升息壓抑非 AI 資本支出，目標本益比由 40 倍調降至 36 倍，以 2026–2027
年平均 EPS 11.8 元，推得目標價 420 元，維持買進評等。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26/4Q26 EPS 估 3.1/3.0 元，下修 5/14%，主因人力產能提升不足。
中國需求維持高檔，漲價需待歐洲市場顯著成長。 
⧫  
考量產能提升受限與歐洲市場仍待復甦 ，估上銀 2026/27 年 EPS 達
10.1/13.5 元，下修 6%/10%。 
⧫  
AI 帶動自動化需求回升， 然短期待歐洲市場復甦帶動第二波漲價， 以
2026-27 年平均 EPS 與 36 倍本益比得目標價 420 元，買進評等。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子設備 2026 年 10 月 15 日 Hiwin 第2 頁，共 18頁  
營運分析  
圖 1：上銀產品組合 
分類 2025 年佔比 主要產品及用途 
線性滑軌 62% 
支撐和引導運動部件按給定的方向做往返直線運動，應用範圍廣泛，包含電子、電腦
機械、半導體設備、輸送搬運機器、工具機、自動化工程設備、醫療設備以及LCD
製程設備等產業。 
滾珠螺桿 20% 
利用內部的鋼珠的旋轉運動轉換成直線運動的機械元件，主要提供工具機XYZ 軸的
直線運動需求。 
工業機器人 9% 單軸機器人、機器手臂、半導體晶圓移載設備等等。 
其他 9% 
產品包含減速機、軸承、旋轉工作台、刀具、海外子公司銷售大銀微系統馬達等線軌
與螺桿相關零配件。 
資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 
3Q26 EPS 估 3.1 元，下修 5%主因營收規模略低於預期，受限人力產能提升不足 
上銀 3Q26 營收 81.1 億元，季增 8%/年增 35%，低於本中心預期 5%，主因其人力產能提升不足且
新招工產能學習曲線須時間提升。營收季增主因來自 1H26 漲價效應顯現。整體 2H26 持續受惠中
國十五五規劃帶動工具機產業升級、 AI 資料中心與機器人帶動螺桿 /線軌等自動化零組件需求成長。
歐洲市場需求持續回升，其 PMI 以連續 8 個月維持擴張態勢且續創新高。此外，半導體自動化設備
持續出貨挹注。毛利率估 36.0%，季增 1.8 個 ppts/年增 7.4 個 ppts；營益率 16.6%，季增 2.8 個
ppts/年增 9.8 個 ppts，均下修 0.1 個 ppts，主因營收規模較預期低。EPS 估達 3.1 元，季增 28%/
年增 143%，下修 5%。

4Q26 EPS 估 3.0 元，下修 14%，推測為產能提升不足且需求須待歐洲市場復甦 
估上銀 4Q26 營收 81.3 億元，季持平，下修 14%，主因公司人力產能提升不足且新招工產能學習
曲線時間仍須提升；此外，本中心推估儘管中國市場持續受惠十五五規劃帶動工具機產業升級、 AI
資料中心與機器人帶動螺桿 /線軌等自動化零組件需求成長，然歐洲市場需求未見顯著成長， (過往
擴張時期 PMI 達 55-65，仍高於 9M26 52.9)，使公司於短期產能擴張速度較過去上升循環為保守。
毛利率估 35.3%，季減 0.7/年增 8.3 個 ppts；營益率 17.2%，季增 0.6 個 ppts/年增 12.4 個 ppts，
均維持前次預估。EPS 估達 3.0 元，季減 4%/年增 125%，下修 14%。
